HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B004","root_001457/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1a1fa2ebbf73d004b5d1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:2:2\",\"root_001110\",[\"B004\"]],[\"111:2:4\",\"root_001457\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Yerleşme çağrışımı, malın kişiye kalıcı bir güvenlik ve sabit yer sağlayamadığı mekânsal bir tersine dönüş önerir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_31511b4509495ef4a1bf","text":"3. **Accumulation reverses into combustible liability.** **Lexical evidence:** `مَالُهُ`, root `م و ل`, branch `B001`, is property amassed as possession; `كَسَبَ`, root `ك س ب`, branch `B001`, is benefit deliberately obtained. Later `حَطَب`, root `ح ط ب`, branch `B001`, is material gathered as fuel, and `ص ل ي`, branch `B004`, supplies kindling/fire-use. **Structure:** acquisition vocabulary is followed by a concrete supply sequence: fire, a carrier, gathered fuel, and carrying cord. **Role and abduction:** the packet supplies accumulation and fuel collection; I infer the conversion arrow by which gathered means cease to shield and instead become inputs to the destructive system. **Reading change:** the negation exposes an inversion of value: what counts as gain at acquisition can count as fuel at consequence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001296/B001","root_001457/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4181d5e84f69a119119b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:2:4\",\"root_001457\",[\"B001\"]],[\"111:2:6\",\"root_001296\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Birikmiş mal ve kazanç, sonraki ateş ve odun dizisinde koruyucu sermaye olmaktan çıkıp yıkımı besleyen yanıcı girdiye dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001296/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_424ec6afa0df09658c2a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:2:6\",\"root_001296\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Avcı kazancı çağrışımı ile son bağın tuzak işlevi birleştirildiğinde, elde eden kişinin kendi edinme düzenince yakalandığı düşünülebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000335/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6adbaae6d3c13921010a","text":"- 111:4 surprised the reading of `كَسَبَ`: because `حَمَّالَةَ ٱلْحَطَبِ` can carry physical fuel and harmful speech (`ح ط ب` B001/B003), \"what he earned\" no longer reads as a ledger item only. It becomes whatever his agency has put into circulation for others to carry. - 111:5 made the protection problem more concrete. `ح ب ل` B002 can be covenant, protection, and connection, but it ends as a rope on the neck. That retrospectively clarifies why `أَغْنَىٰ` B002 is denied: no substitute, bond, or guarantor successfully stands between him and the consequence. - 111:3 also turned property into combustible matter. The movement from `مَالُهُ`/`كَسَبَ` to fire and then to firewood made accumulation itself available to be reread as fuel.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_71715c8d07aa57574f43","text":"1. **Neither stock nor activity can stand in for the person.** **Lexical evidence:** `أَغْنَى` has root `غ ن ي`, branch `B002` (sufficing, availing, sparing, or replacing); `مَالُهُ` has root `م و ل`, branch `B001` (acquired wealth); and `كَسَبَ` has root `ك س ب`, branch `B001` (seeking and obtaining gain). **Structure:** the repeated negator `مَا ... وَمَا` balances stored property against productive action, while `عَنْهُ` places both in a failed substitutive relation to him. `111:3` then retains him as the future subject who encounters the fire. **Role and abduction:** grammar and branch scopes supply the candidates for substitution; I infer that the fire's unchanged subject proves their failure to take his place or spare him. **Reading change:** `أَغْنَى عَنْهُ` means more than “did not help”: nothing owned or performed can become an effective surrogate.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7d14f87d31661b8e81b6","text":"4. **Exploratory: the earner becomes the caught quarry.** **Lexical evidence:** root `ك س ب`, branch `B003`, calls predatory or hunting creatures “earners”; `111:5` supplies `حَبْل`, root `ح ب ل`, branch `B005` (a snare that catches), while `يَدَا` in `111:1`, root `ي د ي`, branch `B001`, includes a hand caught in a snare. **Structure:** the packet begins with active hands, moves through earning, and ends with bodily entanglement. **Role and abduction:** hunting and snaring images are lexically supplied, but I infer the reversal from acquirer/predator to captured household. This remains exploratory because `كَسَبَ` is not itself a hunting construction. **Reading change:** earning can be heard as a capture strategy whose terminal effect is to capture its own agentive system.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B002","root_001296/B001","root_001457/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_823c9dab70cf3ecf3035","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:2:2\",\"root_001110\",[\"B002\"]],[\"111:2:4\",\"root_001457\",[\"B001\"]],[\"111:2:6\",\"root_001296\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Mal ve kazanım, kişinin yerine geçecek bir bedel, güvence ya da koruyucu bağ olamaz; sonuç yine doğrudan ona ulaşır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9fef9a723c4c8d3ed67b","text":"2. **Marital sufficiency is included in the failed household economy.** **Lexical evidence:** root `غ ن ي`, branch `B006`, explicitly includes marriage as sufficiency or protection; the later `وَٱمْرَأَتُهُ` supplies the spousal relation, while `حَمَّالَةَ` (`ح م ل`, branch `B004`) can denote guarantee or indemnity. **Structure:** the wife enters immediately after the man's fire sentence, not as an external rescuer but as another participant bearing a load. **Role and abduction:** marriage and surety are supplied branch possibilities; I infer that the negated sufficiency extends from money into household protection, and that her “guaranteeing” role only joins her to the liability. **Reading change:** the ayah denies not just financial insulation but the capacity of the marital unit to indemnify its central figure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B004","root_001457/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ad92788de39a6aea0801","text":"4. **Settled dwelling is made homeless by non-avail.** Branch evidence: `غ ن ي` B004, dwelling/staying long/former habitations; `م و ل` B001, property; later `ح م ل` B001, transport and outward load; `ح ب ل` B001, leading/binding rope. Structural cue: the wealth statement stands between the perishing person and a moving/carrying scene. Assigned role: the dwelling branch tests whether wealth creates a settled place. Abductive move: I infer a spatial reversal: what seemed to establish him becomes unable to keep him in place. Reading change: the denial of `أَغْنَى` also reads as loss of settled security, not only failure of payment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b790a9ccaad2446a4019","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:2:2\",\"root_001110\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Eşin sonraki yük taşıyan konumu, geçersiz kalan yeterliliğin evlilik ve hane korumasını da kapsadığını gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000335/B001","root_000880/B004","root_001296/B001","root_001457/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d6d5bb848480ff45763a","text":"2. **What he earned becomes feedstock instead of defense.** Branch evidence: `ك س ب` B001, seeking/obtaining livelihood or benefit; `م و ل` B001, property; later `ح ط ب` B001, firewood gathered as fuel; `ل ه ب` B001, kindled flame; `ص ل ي` B004, kindling/fuel/fire-straightening. Structural cue: gain and property precede the firewood carrier and the flame. Assigned role: earnings supply the acquisition-side of a fuel economy. Abductive move: I supply the transformation arrow: accumulations that were meant to benefit are converted by the later imagery into combustible material. Reading change: \"what he earned\" is no longer neutral profit; it can be read as stored fuel for the fire-system.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001296/B001","root_001296/B002","root_001693/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d77bfa11a18528554542","text":"3. **The hands' deeds are separated from the person and then denied efficacy.** Branch evidence: earlier `ي د ي` B009, hands as one's act; `ي د ي` B004, possession; `ك س ب` B001, self-acquisition; `ك س ب` B002, bringing gain to someone else. Structural cue: 111:1 isolates the hands; 111:2 names his property and acquired output; 111:4 shows another person bearing. Assigned role: `كَسَبَ` gathers both personal earning and what one causes to be gained or carried by another. Abductive move: I infer that the wife-carrier later makes \"what he earned\" socially distributed rather than purely private. Reading change: the ayah reads as judgment on a network of acts and induced acts, not only on private assets.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001296/B001","root_001296/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e155e7afe33419425341","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:2:6\",\"root_001296\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kazanılan şey, yalnız kişisel varlık değil, başkasına taşıttırılan ve dolaşıma sokulan eylemlerin sonucu olarak da genişler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e64e11b42b651b0cb2d7","text":"`111:4` unexpectedly activated `غ ن ي` branch `B006`: the explicit wife made marriage itself one of the sufficiency/protection systems negated by `أَغْنَى`, while her load made that system a liability rather than a refuge. `111:3–4` also corrected “wealth did not help” into a value-reversal, because acquisition is followed by gathered fuel. Finally, the snaring cord of `111:5` made the otherwise remote hunting-creature branch of `ك س ب` (`B003`) visible as an exploratory predator-to-captive reversal.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B001","root_001110/B002","root_001457/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f4c6391a434d72aeec69","text":"1. **Wealth fails specifically as substitute protection.** Branch evidence: `غ ن ي` B002, sufficing/availing/replacing; `غ ن ي` B001, wealth and self-sufficiency; `م و ل` B001, acquiring and having property; later `ح ب ل` B002, covenant/protection/connection; `ح م ل` B004, guarantee and indemnity. Structural cue: the denial of availing is followed by a spouse who carries and a rope that may signify protection or bond. Assigned role: wealth is tested as a would-be replacement shield, pledge, or guarantor. Abductive move: I infer that social-legal protection images later in the packet activate what wealth was expected to do. Reading change: the ayah is not simply \"money did not help\"; it says the whole economy of substitution, guarantee, and protective bond collapses.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only. HFT-stated segmentation, word indices, roots, branches, and roles remain attributed nominations unless independently supplied elsewhere in this packet.","cited_anchor_count":0,"exact_arabic_is_surface_evidence_only":true,"missing_anchor_refs":[],"supplied_anchor_count":0,"target_morphology_supplied":false},"branch_refs":[],"payload":"The hand and cutting branches recast wealth and acquisition as a post-collapse balance sheet of disabled agency.","qualification":{"anchor_scope_complete":true,"authoring_effect":"Source qualifications affect epistemic status and containment, never visibility or presumptive outcome.","packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity_status":"legacy_unbound","record_warnings":["Unanchored reader synthesis must be assessed as one inventory item, not accepted as a surah thesis."]},"role":"hft_nomination_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_hft_a:reader_summary:strongest_changes/0","source_type":"hft","support_id":"sup_9c537a000c4fb1abfb9b","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
